# Targeted energy discount: results receipt

Great Britain, FY2027-28. Generated by `uk-energy-reforms run`. Datasets:

- `efrs_1573`: Enhanced FRS 2024-25 (policyengine-uk-data 1.57.3) (policyengine/policyengine-uk-data-private@25af520a, sha256 ef34c1ae2821…). Energy spend imputed from LCFS and raked to NEED 2023 at Ofgem Q2-2026 unit rates. Weights are highly concentrated (Kish ESS about 1,100) and sum to 30.7m GB households in 2026-27, about 7% above Microcosm (28.6m) and 10% above RF's 28m, so its counts run high.
- `microcosm_979`: Microcosm UK 2024-25 (staged #979 national attempt) (policyengine/populace-uk-private@f6df65b1, sha256 c5f107ab6eaf…). Energy spend priced with DESNZ QEP FY2024-25, raked to NEED, levelled to Energy Trends. Staged candidate, not a certified release.
